that there exists a
{\mathbb{P}}_{\nu}^{\rm FV}-
null event
N\in{\mathcal{F}}_{t^{\star}}
such that for all
\omega\in\Omega\,\backslash\,N
,
\left(M_{t}^{{\rm{id}}}(h(\omega))\left(\widetilde{\omega}\right)\right)_{0\leqslant t\leqslant T}
is a
{\mathbb{P}}_{\omega}\left(\mathrm{d}\widetilde{\omega}\right)-
martingale. We deduce from

[
30
,
Theorem 3.18 of Section 5.3 (p 307)
]
that
{\mathbb{P}}_{\nu}^{\rm FV}-
almost every
\omega\in\Omega
,

\widetilde{\omega}_{|_{[0,t^{\star}]}}=\omega_{|_{[0,t^{\star}]}},\qquad{\mathbb{Q}}_{\omega}\left(\mathrm{d}\widetilde{\omega}\right)\!-\!{\rm{a.s.}}

(C.4)

This implies that,
{\mathbb{Q}}_{\omega}\left(\mathrm{d}\widetilde{\omega}\right)-
almost surely,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}_{t}\left(\widetilde{\omega}\right)=M^{{\rm{id}}}_{t}\left(h(\omega)\right)\left(\widetilde{\omega}\right)-M^{{\rm{id}}}_{t\wedge t^{\star}}\left(h(\omega)\right)\left(\widetilde{\omega}\right)

\displaystyle=\left\{\begin{array}[]{lcc}M^{{\rm{id}}}_{t-t^{\star}}\left(h\left(\omega\right)\right)\left(\theta_{t^{\star}}\left(\widetilde{\omega}\right)\right)&{\rm{if}}&t>t^{\star}\\
0&{\rm{if}}&t\leqslant t^{\star}\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle=M^{{\rm{id}}}_{\left(t-t^{\star}\right)^{+}}\left(h\left(\omega\right)\right)\left(\theta_{t^{\star}}\left(\widetilde{\omega}\right)\right)

where
(a)^{+}
designates the non-negative part of
a\in{\mathbb{R}}
.
Let
n\in{\mathbb{N}}^{\star}
and
0\leqslant s\leqslant T
. To prove the martingale property for all
{\mathcal{F}}_{s}-
measurable bounded random variable
Z
, it is sufficient to prove it on elementary events. Then, we consider a random variable
Z
of the form

Z(\omega):={\mathds{1}}_{\left\{\omega_{t_{1}}\in\Gamma_{1},\cdots,\omega_{t_{n}}\in\Gamma_{n}\right\}}

where for all
i\in\{1,\cdots,n\},t_{i}\leqslant s
and
\Gamma_{i}\subset{\mathcal{M}}_{1}({\mathbb{R}})
measurable. We define

\widetilde{Z}\left(\omega,\widetilde{\omega}\right):={\mathds{1}}_{\left\{\omega_{t_{i}}\in\Gamma_{i},\forall i\in\{1,\cdots,n\}\ {\rm{such\ that}}\ t_{i}\leqslant t^{\star}\right\}}{\mathds{1}}_{\left\{\tilde{\omega}_{t_{j}}\in\Gamma_{j},\forall j\in\{1,\cdots,n\}\ {\rm{such\ that}}\ t_{j}>t^{\star}\right\}}.

By (
C.4
),
\widetilde{Z}\left(\omega,\widetilde{\omega}\right)=Z\left(\omega\right)
,
{\mathbb{Q}}_{\omega}\left(\mathrm{d}\widetilde{\omega}\right)-{\rm{a.s.}}
Therefore, for
{\mathbb{P}}_{\nu}^{\rm FV}-
almost every
\omega\in\Omega
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathbb{Q}}_{\omega}}\left(\left[{\mathcal{M}}_{t}-{\mathcal{M}}_{s}\right]Z\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt={\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathbb{Q}}_{\omega}\left(\mathrm{d}\widetilde{\omega}\right)}\left(\left[M_{\left(t-t^{\star}\right)^{+}}^{{\rm{id}}}\left(h\left(\omega\right)\right)\left(\theta_{t^{\star}}\left(\widetilde{\omega}\right)\right)-M_{\left(s-t^{\star}\right)^{+}}^{{\rm{id}}}\left(h\left(\omega\right)\right)\left(\theta_{t^{\star}}\left(\widetilde{\omega}\right)\right)\right]\widetilde{Z}\left(\omega,\widetilde{\omega}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt={\mathds{1}}_{\left\{\omega_{t_{i}}\in\Gamma_{i},\forall i\in\{1,\cdots,n\}\ {\rm{such\ that}}\ t_{i}\leqslant t^{\star}\right\}}\times

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathbb{Q}}_{\omega}\left(\mathrm{d}\tilde{\omega}\right)}\left(\left[M_{t-t^{\star}}^{{\rm{id}}}\left(h\left(\omega\right)\right)\left(\theta_{t^{\star}}\left(\widetilde{\omega}\right)\right)-M_{s-t^{\star}}^{{\rm{id}}}\left(h\left(\omega\right)\right)\left(\theta_{t^{\star}}\left(\widetilde{\omega}\right)\right)\right]^{\phantom{1}}\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 99.58464pt\times\left.{\mathds{1}}_{\left\{\left[\theta_{t^{\star}}\left(\tilde{\omega}\right)\right]_{t_{j}-t^{\star}}\in\Gamma_{j},\forall j\in\{1,\cdots,n\}\ {\rm{such\ that}}\ t_{j}>t^{\star}\right\}}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\overset{{(\ref{PPP_omega})}}{=}{\mathds{1}}_{\left\{\omega_{t_{i}}\in\Gamma_{i},\forall i\in\{1,\cdots,n\}\ {\rm{such\ that}}\ t_{i}\leqslant t^{\star}\right\}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathbb{P}}_{\omega}\left(\mathrm{d}\hat{\omega}\right)}\left(\left[M_{t-t^{\star}}^{{\rm{id}}}\left(h\left(\omega\right)\right)\left(\widehat{\omega}\right)-M_{s-t^{\star}}^{{\rm{id}}}\left(h\left(\omega\right)\right)\left(\widehat{\omega}\right)\right]^{\phantom{1}}\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 99.58464pt\times\left.{\mathds{1}}_{\left\{\hat{\omega}_{t_{j}-t^{\star}}\in\Gamma_{j},\forall j\in\{1,\cdots,n\}\ {\rm{such\ that}}\ t_{j}>t^{\star}\right\}}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=0,

using that
\left(M_{t-t^{\star}}^{{\rm{id}}}\left(h\left(\omega\right)\right)\left(\widehat{\omega}\right)\right)_{t^{\star}\leqslant t\leqslant T+t^{\star}}
is a
{\mathbb{P}}_{\omega}\left(\mathrm{d}\widehat{\omega}\right)-
martingale if the internal indicator is non zero. Thus, for
{\mathbb{P}}_{\nu}^{\rm FV}-
almost every
\omega\in\Omega
,
\left({\mathcal{M}}_{t}\left(\widetilde{\omega}\right)\right)_{0\leqslant t\leqslant T}
is a
{\mathbb{Q}}_{\omega}\left(\mathrm{d}\widetilde{\omega}\right)-
martingale which completes the first part of this proof. In similar way, we can prove

M^{{\rm{id}}^{2}}_{t}\left(h\left(\widetilde{\omega}\right)\right)\left(\widetilde{\omega}\right)-M^{{\rm{id}}^{2}}_{t\wedge t^{\star}}\left(h\left(\widetilde{\omega}\right)\right)\left(\widetilde{\omega}\right)

is a
{\mathbb{P}}_{\nu}^{\rm FV}-
martingale. Applying
Itô
’s formula to compute
\left\langle h\left(\widetilde{\omega}_{t}\right),\widetilde{\omega}_{t}\right\rangle^{2}
and comparing it to the previous result, we obtain the announced result.
\square
